## Notes for the C12 audit

- Region names and the access kinds are produced by MemberTrace: peersA/maskA/playersA (session object
  session 0 offset 0x60..0x226C) and headerB/peersB/maskB/playersB (0x226C..0x4538).
- `0x44FF4D|peersA` is the hot accessor (tens of thousands of hits per dump); `0x44EA95..0x44EADD`
  walk `playersA` fields in 0x10 steps (0x1780-0x17D0).
- Hit counts are cumulative per distinct instruction between dumps; the dumps repeat every 30 s, so the
  same instruction appears once per dump with its running count.

## Run 3 (dedicated) - 0 MEMBER| lines

The dedicated-server path does not currently produce a joinable session or any trace output:

- noble-dedicated.exe (13:07 build) does not contain MemberTrace at all (no "MemberTrace: tracing" string;
  the target's project was last configured before src/host/MemberTrace.cpp was added to CMakeLists).
- DedicatedServer::Start only forwards UPnP ports, starts the RCON listener and an empty 60 Hz sleep loop; it
  never pumps haloreach.dll frames and never creates a game session, so there is no host for a client to join.
- A 40 s run with -trace-members -limits v16 logged only the banner, "Engine limits: requested Extended (V16)",
  "Dedicated server active!" and exited with 0xC0000409 on quit; no MEMBER| lines.

## Run 3 (client) - not run

No host existed to join (see Run 3 (dedicated)), so the client run was not started and produced no MEMBER| lines.
